# Skills · T · part 54

872 entries, thinking-in-systems to thread-finalizer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **thinking-in-systems**: A systems-thinking skill based on "Thinking in Systems" (Donella Meadows). Draw stock-and-flow diagrams and feedback loops, diagnose "why fixing it makes it worse" (watching only flows, ignoring delays, treating symptoms, eroding goals), and use the 12 leverage points to locate the level of intervention. Treat workflows and projects as systems. Use when the user says "the change had no effect", "the metric won't move", "where's the leverage point", "is this a vicious cycle", "what do we do about a systemic problem" (at the level of organization / process / metrics); also for analyzing process… ([Trustbaselab/trustbase-skills](https://github.com/Trustbaselab/trustbase-skills/tree/HEAD/cognitive/thinking-in-systems) · ★1)
- **thinking-in-systems**: Apply Donella Meadows's systems thinking to recurring problems involving stocks, flows, feedback, delays, or conflicting incentives. Use for persistent backlogs, capacity problems, policy resistance, and unintended consequences; ordinary isolated fixes do not require a system model. ([wangchuan2003-a11y/book-to-action-skills](https://github.com/wangchuan2003-a11y/book-to-action-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/thinking-in-systems) · ★1)

- **thinking-threatmodel**: Tests ideas, decisions, and strategies across multiple time horizons and threat scenarios. Inspired by Daniel Miessler's WorldThreatModelHarness. [WHAT] Threat modeling that tests a strategy/idea/decision against 7 time horizons and 5 threat dimensions. Produces a vulnerability matrix and recommendations. [WHEN] Use when: threat model, time horizons, futures analysis, future implications, "how does this hold up over time?", "what happens if..." [EXPERTISE] Threat modeling, scenario analysis, temporal risk assessment, futures thinking ([carlheath/ogmios](https://github.com/carlheath/ogmios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/thinking-threatmodel) · ★9)

- **thinking-token-efficient**: use when a complex chat task needs adaptive private reasoning effort, token-efficient tool planning, code or artifact analysis, multi-step synthesis, evidence work, or validation without unnecessary reasoning. do not use for simple direct answers, ordinary rewriting or translation, visible prose compression, image generation, raw chain-of-thought disclosure, or shortcuts that reduce correctness, evidence, citations, validation, safety, compatibility, or required detail. - **thinking-toolkit**: 当用户评估项目想法或可行性、纠结技术选型或二选一方案、想深入研究某个公司/行业/股票/加密货币/期货标的、要核查某个市场说法或数据真伪、想法多但没行动需要最小成本试错、方案或架构被惯例卡死想推倒重来、想拆解优秀产品或竞品好在哪时使用。12种结构化思考方法工具箱(整理自卡兹克Prompt合集):苏格拉底式提问、双层解释、反向拆解、横纵分析、事实核查、专家会诊、第一性原理、跨领域借解、双向钢人论证、最小实验、天赋挖掘、人生设计。用户表达模糊困惑、想学习或研究某个事物、面临复杂难题犹豫不决、想探索天赋或人生方向,或直接点名以上任一方法时也适用。Triggers: 帮我把这个想法捋一捋，我总感觉哪里不对但说不上来；这个说法/这个数据是真的吗，帮我核查一下；我在两个方案之间犹豫很久了，帮我分析分析；我想系统研究一下这家公司/这个行业；看到一个好产品，帮我拆解它好在哪；想法很多但一直没行动，怎么小成本试一下；帮我找到我的天赋和优势；我对人生方向很迷茫，不知道接下来怎么走。 ([EtherealMingo/university-teacher-skills](https://github.com/EtherealMingo/university-teacher-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/learning-education/thinking-toolkit) · ★22)
- **thinking-toolkit**: Apply a named Thinking Toolkit model or audit an argument with /logic. Use only when the user explicitly invokes the toolkit, names an included model or alias, or invokes /logic. ([ponomr/thinking-toolkit](https://github.com/ponomr/thinking-toolkit/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★22)
- **thinking-toolkit**: 思考决策工具箱元技能——整合 superforecaster（校准预测）、decision-matrix（决策矩阵）、 bayesian-reasoning（贝叶斯推理）、systems-thinking（系统思维）、scout-mindset-bias-check （认知偏见审查）、doctor-strange（平行宇宙沙盘推演）六大思考框架。 覆盖预测→决策→推理→系统分析→偏见审查→情景模拟的完整深度思考链路。 Trigger keywords: 思考工具, 决策工具, 深度思考, 预测, 决策矩阵, 贝叶斯推理, 系统思维, 偏见审查, 沙盘推演, thinking toolkit, superforecaster, decision matrix, bayesian reasoning, systems thinking, scout mindset, doctor strange, 怎么做决定, 帮我分析利弊, 预测一下, 模拟未来, 认知偏见, 理性决策。 ([ZhouYinLong-lab/Dog-Skills](https://github.com/ZhouYinLong-lab/Dog-Skills/tree/HEAD/thinking-toolkit) · ★7)
- **thinking-toolkit**: 当用户评估项目想法或可行性、纠结技术选型或二选一方案、想深入研究某个公司/行业/股票/加密货币/期货标的、要核查某个市场说法或数据真伪、想法多但没行动需要最小成本试错、方案或架构被惯例卡死想推倒重来、想拆解优秀产品或竞品好在哪时使用。12种结构化思考方法工具箱(整理自卡兹克Prompt合集):苏格拉底式提问、双层解释、反向拆解、横纵分析、事实核查、专家会诊、第一性原理、跨领域借解、双向钢人论证、最小实验、天赋挖掘、人生设计。用户表达模糊困惑、想学习或研究某个事物、面临复杂难题犹豫不决、想探索天赋或人生方向,或直接点名以上任一方法时也适用。 ([Rogers-F/thinking-toolkit](https://github.com/Rogers-F/thinking-toolkit/tree/HEAD/thinking-toolkit) · ★3)
